# Baseline changes (view of CHANGES.json)

Machine ledger: `tests/baseline/CHANGES.json`. A test name that is in `original/<suite>.list` and not in the current discovery
must have an entry (`old`, `new` or `replacement[]`, `reason`, `decision` = decision id or owner grant) whose replacement
exists in the current list; prose alone hides nothing. `original/` is immutable.
